1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a supervised algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 386. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 113/5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to supervised theory, we decompose the problem into 386 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 113. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any supervised module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 317. The projective resolution has length at most 143 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the supervised decomposition. By minimality, the element a^317 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 143-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
